				4212 = { gme_leshan_giant_buddha_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4212 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = gme_leshan_giant_buddha
						tier = 3
					}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4212 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = gme_leshan_giant_buddha3
							duration = -1
						}
						remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					4212 = { gme_leshan_giant_buddha_modifier_trigger = no }
					4212 = {
						owner = {
							has_country_flag = dependencylost
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha1
				remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha2
				remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = subjectmonumentlost
				OR ={
					has_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha1
					has_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha2
					has_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha3
				}
			}
			remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha1
			remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha2
			remove_country_modifier = gme_leshan_giant_buddha3
		}
	}
}
